================ FILE: src\app\globals.css ================

/* Las fuentes (Inter, Montserrat, JetBrains Mono) se cargan con <link> en src/app/layout.tsx. */

@import "tailwindcss";

/* Modo oscuro por clase (Tailwind v4): se activa con <html class="dark"> */
@custom-variant dark (&:where(.dark, .dark *));

@theme {
  --color-ug-navy: #002E5D;
  --color-ug-institutional: #00468B;
  --color-ug-gold: #B38E5D;
  --color-ug-light: #F4F6F9;
  --color-ug-text: #1E293B;
  --color-ug-text-secondary: #475569;
  --color-ug-tech: #0284C7;

  /* Paleta oscura institucional */
  --color-ug-night: #090D16;
  --color-ug-slate: #0F172A;
  --color-ug-surface: #162032;
  --color-ug-line: #1E293B;
  --color-ug-gold-bright: #D4AF37;
  --color-ug-sky: #38BDF8;
  --color-ug-sky-soft: #93C5FD;
  --color-ug-ink: #F8FAFC;
  --color-ug-ink-soft: #CBD5E1;

  --font-inter: "Inter", sans-serif;
  --font-montserrat: "Montserrat", sans-serif;
  --font-jetbrains-mono: "JetBrains Mono", monospace;

  --radius: 0.5rem;
}

@layer base {
  /* En modo oscuro, los tokens de texto y de fondo alterno cambian solos. */
  .dark {
    color-scheme: dark;
    --color-ug-text: #F8FAFC;
    --color-ug-text-secondary: #CBD5E1;
    --color-ug-light: #0F172A;
  }

  * {
    box-sizing: border-box;
    border-color: #e2e8f0;
  }

  .dark * {
    border-color: #1E293B;
  }

  body {
    background-color: #FFFFFF;
    color: var(--color-ug-text);
    font-family: var(--font-inter);
    font-feature-settings: "rlig" 1, "calt" 1;
    margin: 0;
    /* Evita scroll horizontal en celulares sin romper el Navbar sticky. */
    overflow-x: hidden;
    overflow-x: clip;
  }

  .dark body {
    background-color: #090D16;
  }

  html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
  }
}

/*
  Importante: aquí ya NO se redefinen .text-ug-navy, .bg-ug-gold, etc.
  Tailwind v4 las genera desde @theme, y duplicarlas en @layer utilities
  hacía que ganaran sobre las variantes dark:.
*/
@layer utilities {
  .font-montserrat {
    font-family: var(--font-montserrat);
  }

  .font-inter {
    font-family: var(--font-inter);
  }

  .font-mono {
    font-family: var(--font-jetbrains-mono);
  }

  .pleca-oro {
    position: relative;
  }

  .pleca-oro::after {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    bottom: 0;
    width: 4rem;
    height: 4px;
    background-color: #B38E5D;
  }
}

================ FILE: src\app\layout.tsx ================

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Inter, Montserrat, JetBrains_Mono } from "next/font/google";
import "./globals.css";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/layout/TopBar";
import { Navbar } from "@/components/layout/Navbar";
import { Footer } from "@/components/layout/Footer";
import { AdminBar } from "@/components/admin/AdminBar";
import { AdminDrawer } from "@/components/admin/AdminDrawer";
import { LabProvider } from "@/context/LabContext";

const inter = Inter({
  subsets: ["latin"],
  variable: "--font-inter",
  display: "swap",
});

const montserrat = Montserrat({
  subsets: ["latin"],
  variable: "--font-montserrat",
  weight: ["400", "600", "700", "800"],
  display: "swap",
});

const jetbrainsMono = JetBrains_Mono({
  subsets: ["latin"],
  variable: "--font-jetbrains-mono",
  weight: ["400", "500"],
  display: "swap",
});

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Laboratorio de Sistemas Bio-inspirados | Universidad de Guanajuato",
  description:
    "Centro de investigación en Robótica, Electrónica y Neurociencia. Laboratorio de Sistemas Bio-inspirados (LISB-UG).",
  keywords: [
    "LISB-UG",
    "Laboratorio de Sistemas Bio-inspirados",
    "Universidad de Guanajuato",
    "Robótica",
    "Electrónica",
    "Neurociencia",
    "Bio-señales",
  ],
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
}>) {
  return (
    <html lang="es" className={inter.className}>
      <head>
        <link rel="icon" href="/favicon.ico" />
      </head>
      <body className={`${inter.variable} ${montserrat.variable} ${jetbrainsMono.variable} antialiased`}>
        <LabProvider>
          <TopBar />
          <Navbar />
          {children}
          <Footer />
          <AdminBar />
          <AdminDrawer />
        </LabProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

================ FILE: src\components\layout\Navbar.tsx ================

"use client";

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Menu, X } from "lucide-react";
import { LogoMark } from "@/components/ui/LogoMark";
import { ThemeToggle } from "@/components/layout/ThemeToggle";
import { useLab } from "@/context/LabContext";
import { isExternalLink } from "@/lib/labHelpers";

export function Navbar() {
  const { labData } = useLab();
  const [isMobileMenuOpen, setIsMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const settings = labData?.settings;

  // Toda sección extra visible aparece sola en el menú.
  // Si tiene "nombre corto" se usa ese; si no, el título.
  const customLinks = (labData?.customSections ?? [])
    .filter((s) => s.visible)
    .map((s) => ({
      id: s.id,
      label: (s.navLabel ?? "").trim() || (s.title ?? "").trim(),
      href: `#${s.id}`,
    }))
    .filter((link) => link.label.length > 0);
  const links = [...(settings?.navLinks ?? []), ...customLinks];

  const linkProps = (href: string) =>
    isExternalLink(href) ? { target: "_blank", rel: "noopener noreferrer" } : {};

  // Cierra el menú móvil al tocar cualquier enlace y hace scroll suave hacia la sección.
  const handleLinkClick = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>, href: string) => {
    setIsMobileMenuOpen(false);
    if (!href.startsWith("#") || href.length < 2) return;
    const target = document.getElementById(href.slice(1));
    if (!target) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
    window.history.replaceState(null, "", href);
  };

  return (
    <nav className="sticky top-0 z-50 w-full bg-white dark:bg-ug-night shadow-sm dark:shadow-none border-b-[3px] border-[#B38E5D]">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between h-16 gap-2 sm:gap-4">
          {/* Logo + nombre */}
          <a href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1 lg:flex-none">
            {settings && (
              <>
                <LogoMark
                  src={labData?.images.logo}
                  alt={`Logo de ${settings.labName}`}
                  className="w-9 h-9 sm:w-10 sm:h-10"
                />
                <div className="hidden sm:block w-0.5 h-8 bg-ug-gold shrink-0" />
                <div className="leading-tight min-w-0">
                  <div className="text-ug-navy dark:text-ug-sky-soft font-montserrat font-bold text-sm sm:text-base xl:text-xl tracking-tight line-clamp-2 sm:line-clamp-1 break-words">
                    {settings.labName}
                  </div>
                  {settings.institutionalSubtitle && (
                    <div className="hidden sm:block text-xs text-ug-text-secondary tracking-wide truncate">
                      {settings.institutionalSubtitle}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </a>

          {/* Enlaces escritorio */}
          {settings && (
            <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-6 shrink-0">
              {links.map((link) => (
                <a
                  key={link.id}
                  href={link.href}
                  {...linkProps(link.href)}
                  onClick={(e) => handleLinkClick(e, link.href)}
                  className="text-ug-navy dark:text-ug-ink font-medium text-sm hover:text-ug-institutional dark:hover:text-ug-gold-bright transition-colors"
                >
                  {link.label}
                </a>
              ))}
              {settings.navButtonLabel && (
                <a
                  href={settings.navButtonHref}
                  {...linkProps(settings.navButtonHref)}
                  onClick={(e) => handleLinkClick(e, settings.navButtonHref)}
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-ug-navy text-white dark:bg-ug-gold dark:text-ug-night text-sm font-semibold rounded-md hover:bg-ug-institutional dark:hover:bg-ug-gold-bright transition-colors"
                >
                  {settings.navButtonLabel}
                </a>
              )}
              <ThemeToggle />
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Controles móvil: tema + hamburguesa (siempre visibles, no se empujan) */}
          <div className="flex lg:hidden items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
            <ThemeToggle />
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen((prev) => !prev)}
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 text-ug-navy dark:text-ug-sky-soft hover:text-ug-institutional dark:hover:text-ug-gold-bright"
              aria-label={isMobileMenuOpen ? "Cerrar menú móvil" : "Abrir menú móvil"}
              aria-expanded={isMobileMenuOpen}
            >
              {isMobileMenuOpen ? (
                <X size={24} strokeWidth={2.5} />
              ) : (
                <Menu size={24} strokeWidth={2.5} />
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Menú móvil desplegable */}
      {isMobileMenuOpen && settings && (
        <div className="lg:hidden bg-white dark:bg-ug-night border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-ug-line max-h-[calc(100dvh-6rem)] overflow-y-auto">
          <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-4 space-y-1">
            {links.map((link) => (
              <a
                key={link.id}
                href={link.href}
                {...linkProps(link.href)}
                onClick={(e) => handleLinkClick(e, link.href)}
                className="block text-ug-navy dark:text-ug-ink text-sm font-medium py-3 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-ug-line hover:text-ug-institutional dark:hover:text-ug-gold-bright transition-colors break-words"
              >
                {link.label}
              </a>
            ))}
            <div className="pt-3 space-y-3">
              <ThemeToggle variant="full" />
              {settings.navButtonLabel && (
                <a
                  href={settings.navButtonHref}
                  {...linkProps(settings.navButtonHref)}
                  onClick={(e) => handleLinkClick(e, settings.navButtonHref)}
                  className="block px-5 py-3 bg-ug-navy text-white dark:bg-ug-gold dark:text-ug-night text-sm font-semibold text-center rounded-md hover:bg-ug-institutional dark:hover:bg-ug-gold-bright transition-colors"
                >
                  {settings.navButtonLabel}
                </a>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </nav>
  );
}

================ FILE: src\context\LabContext.tsx ================

"use client";

import React, {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useState,
  useEffect,
  useCallback,
  useRef,
  ReactNode,
} from "react";
import { labApiClient, LabData } from "@/services/labApiClient";

type ToastState = { message: string; type: "success" | "error" } | null;

interface LabContextType {
  labData: LabData | null;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  token: string | null;
  toast: ToastState;
  adminPanelOpen: boolean;
  loginOpen: boolean;
  openAdminPanel: () => void;
  closeAdminPanel: () => void;
  openLoginModal: () => void;
  closeLoginModal: () => void;
  login: (password: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => void;
  updateLabData: (newData: Partial<LabData>) => Promise<void>;
  uploadImage: (file: File) => Promise<string>;
  resetToDefaults: () => Promise<void>;
  showToast: (message: string, type?: "success" | "error") => void;
}

// Valor por defecto seguro (SSR / componentes fuera del provider)
const defaultLabContext: LabContextType = {
  labData: null,
  loading: true,
  error: null,
  isAdmin: false,
  token: null,
  toast: null,
  adminPanelOpen: false,
  loginOpen: false,
  openAdminPanel: () => {},
  closeAdminPanel: () => {},
  openLoginModal: () => {},
  closeLoginModal: () => {},
  login: async () => {},
  logout: () => {},
  updateLabData: async () => {},
  uploadImage: async () => "",
  resetToDefaults: async () => {},
  showToast: () => {},
};

const LabContext = createContext<LabContextType>(defaultLabContext);

function getErrorMessage(err: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  return err instanceof Error && err.message ? err.message : fallback;
}

export function LabProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [labData, setLabData] = useState<LabData | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = useState(false);
  const [token, setToken] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<ToastState>(null);
  const [adminPanelOpen, setAdminPanelOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loginOpen, setLoginOpen] = useState(false);
  const toastTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  const showToast = useCallback(
    (message: string, type: "success" | "error" = "success") => {
      if (toastTimer.current) clearTimeout(toastTimer.current);
      setToast({ message, type });
      toastTimer.current = setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 4000);
    },
    []
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (toastTimer.current) clearTimeout(toastTimer.current);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    async function loadLabData() {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        const data = await labApiClient.getLabData();
        if (cancelled) return;
        setLabData(data);
        setError(null);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setError(getErrorMessage(err, "Error al cargar datos del laboratorio"));
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      }
    }
    loadLabData();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  const openAdminPanel = useCallback(() => setAdminPanelOpen(true), []);
  const closeAdminPanel = useCallback(() => setAdminPanelOpen(false), []);
  const openLoginModal = useCallback(() => setLoginOpen(true), []);
  const closeLoginModal = useCallback(() => setLoginOpen(false), []);

  const login = useCallback(
    async (password: string) => {
      try {
        const result = await labApiClient.login(password);
        setToken(result.token);
        setIsAdmin(true);
        // Entrada directa al editor: se cierra el login y se abre el panel.
        setLoginOpen(false);
        setAdminPanelOpen(true);
        showToast("Sesión de administrador iniciada", "success");
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        setIsAdmin(false);
        setToken(null);
        showToast(getErrorMessage(err, "Contraseña incorrecta"), "error");
        throw err;
      }
    },
    [showToast]
  );

  const logout = useCallback(() => {
    setToken(null);
    setIsAdmin(false);
    setAdminPanelOpen(false);
    showToast("Sesión cerrada", "success");
  }, [showToast]);

  const updateLabData = useCallback(
    async (newData: Partial<LabData>) => {
      if (!token) {
        throw new Error("No hay sesión de administrador activa");
      }
      try {
        const updated = await labApiClient.updateLabData(newData, token);
        setLabData(updated);
        showToast("Cambios guardados correctamente", "success");
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        showToast(getErrorMessage(err, "Error al guardar cambios"), "error");
        throw err;
      }
    },
    [token, showToast]
  );

  const uploadImage = useCallback(
    async (file: File): Promise<string> => {
      if (!token) {
        throw new Error("No hay sesión de administrador activa");
      }
      try {
        const result = await labApiClient.uploadImage(file, token);
        showToast("Imagen subida correctamente", "success");
        return result.url;
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        showToast(getErrorMessage(err, "Error al subir la imagen"), "error");
        throw err;
      }
    },
    [token, showToast]
  );

  const resetToDefaults = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!token) {
      throw new Error("No hay sesión de administrador activa");
    }
    try {
      const resetData = await labApiClient.resetLabData(token);
      setLabData(resetData);
      showToast("Datos restablecidos a valores iniciales", "success");
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      showToast(getErrorMessage(err, "Error al restablecer datos"), "error");
      throw err;
    }
  }, [token, showToast]);

  return (
    <LabContext.Provider
      value={{
        labData,
        loading,
        error,
        isAdmin,
        token,
        toast,
        adminPanelOpen,
        loginOpen,
        openAdminPanel,
        closeAdminPanel,
        openLoginModal,
        closeLoginModal,
        login,
        logout,
        updateLabData,
        uploadImage,
        resetToDefaults,
        showToast,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </LabContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useLab() {
  return useContext(LabContext);
}

================ FILE: src\context\ThemeContext.tsx ================

"use client";

import React, {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";

export type Theme = "light" | "dark";

export const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = "lisb-theme";

interface ThemeContextType {
  theme: Theme;
  setTheme: (theme: Theme) => void;
  toggleTheme: () => void;
}

const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextType>({
  theme: "light",
  setTheme: () => {},
  toggleTheme: () => {},
});

function applyTheme(theme: Theme) {
  document.documentElement.classList.toggle("dark", theme === "dark");
}

function readStoredTheme(): Theme | null {
  try {
    const value = window.localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY);
    return value === "dark" || value === "light" ? value : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  // El estado inicial es siempre "light" (igual en servidor y cliente) para evitar
  // errores de hidratación. La clase real ya la puso el script del <head>.
  const [theme, setThemeState] = useState<Theme>("light");

  useEffect(() => {
    const media = window.matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
    const stored = readStoredTheme();
    const initial: Theme = stored ?? (media.matches ? "dark" : "light");
    applyTheme(initial);
    setThemeState(initial);

    // Si la persona nunca eligió un tema, se sigue el del sistema en vivo.
    const onSystemChange = (event: MediaQueryListEvent) => {
      if (readStoredTheme() !== null) return;
      const next: Theme = event.matches ? "dark" : "light";
      applyTheme(next);
      setThemeState(next);
    };
    media.addEventListener("change", onSystemChange);
    return () => media.removeEventListener("change", onSystemChange);
  }, []);

  const setTheme = useCallback((next: Theme) => {
    applyTheme(next);
    setThemeState(next);
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, next);
    } catch {
      // Sin localStorage (modo privado, etc.): el tema sigue funcionando en la sesión.
    }
  }, []);

  const toggleTheme = useCallback(() => {
    const isDark = document.documentElement.classList.contains("dark");
    setTheme(isDark ? "light" : "dark");
  }, [setTheme]);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({ theme, setTheme, toggleTheme }),
    [theme, setTheme, toggleTheme]
  );

  return <ThemeContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ThemeContext.Provider>;
}

export function useTheme() {
  return useContext(ThemeContext);
}
